# Skill Index

Comprehensive catalog and source-trust classification for all skills available under `instructions/skills/`. Canonical definitions of the 6-tier trust scale live in [README.md](../../README.md).

## Skill Catalog by Source

### anthropics

| # | Skill | Description |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | [academy-guide](external/anthropics/skills/academy-guide/SKILL.md) | Stop and check this skill before finishing any reply to a question about how to use Claude or a Claude product - it recommends matching courses, tutorials, and use cases from Claude Academy (academy.claude.com), Anthropic's learning hub. Trigger on: "how do I", "how can I", "getting started with", "what can Claude do", "teach me", "learn to use"; questions about artifacts, projects, skills, plugins, connectors, MCP; requests about rolling Claude out to a team, class, or organization; and any ask for training materials, onboarding content, or learning resources. Use it when the user is learning how to use a feature or product - not when they are mid-task and just want the task done. This skill composes with other skills: after consulting product documentation to answer how a Claude feature works, also check here for a matching course or tutorial - a docs-grounded answer and an Academy recommendation belong together. Only recommend on a strong match; never invent Academy content. |
| 2 | [algorithmic-art](external/anthropics/skills/algorithmic-art/SKILL.md) | Creating algorithmic art using p5.js with seeded randomness and interactive parameter exploration. Use this when users request creating art using code, generative art, algorithmic art, flow fields, or particle systems. Create original algorithmic art rather than copying existing artists' work to avoid copyright violations. |
| 3 | [brand-guidelines](external/anthropics/skills/brand-guidelines/SKILL.md) | Applies Anthropic's official brand colors and typography to any sort of artifact that may benefit from having Anthropic's look-and-feel. Use it when brand colors or style guidelines, visual formatting, or company design standards apply. |
| 4 | [canvas-design](external/anthropics/skills/canvas-design/SKILL.md) | Create beautiful visual art in .png and .pdf documents using design philosophy. You should use this skill when the user asks to create a poster, piece of art, design, or other static piece. Create original visual designs, never copying existing artists' work to avoid copyright violations. |
| 5 | [claude-api](external/anthropics/skills/claude-api/SKILL.md) | Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK - model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration. TRIGGER - read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" - whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) - never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens). SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai\|langchain_openai\|google.generativeai\|genai\|mistralai\|cohere\|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named - don't Read the file). |
| 6 | [discernment-nudge](external/anthropics/skills/discernment-nudge/SKILL.md) | After you give a substantive answer or draft that the user may act on - advice or recommendations, drafted artifacts such as goals, plans, pitches, proposals, or emails, estimates or projections, analysis or interpretation of data, factual claims they may rely on, or a multi-step argument - invoke this skill BEFORE finalizing your reply and then, if it applies, append 2-3 short follow-up questions, each tied to something specific in what you just produced, that help the user check key facts, probe the reasoning or assumptions, and notice missing context. Do this at most once per conversation. Skip it when the user asked a trivial how-to or simple lookup, wants a purely educational explanation, asked you only to format, convert, or assemble a file from content they provided, is writing code they will run, is doing creative writing or casual chat, or already asked you to double-check, cite, or review - the skill file explains these boundaries and the exact output format. |
| 7 | [doc-coauthoring](external/anthropics/skills/doc-coauthoring/SKILL.md) | Guide users through a structured workflow for co-authoring documentation. Use when user wants to write documentation, proposals, technical specs, decision docs, or similar structured content. This workflow helps users efficiently transfer context, refine content through iteration, and verify the doc works for readers. Trigger when user mentions writing docs, creating proposals, drafting specs, or similar documentation tasks. |
| 8 | [docx](external/anthropics/skills/docx/SKILL.md) | Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx files) or Word templates (.dotx files). Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, performing find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file, use this skill. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or general coding tasks unrelated to document generation. |

| 1 | [academic-plotting](external/AI-Research-SKILLs/20-ml-paper-writing/academic-plotting/SKILL.md) | Generates publication-quality figures for ML papers from research context. Given experiment results or data, auto-selects chart type and generates data-driven figures via matplotlib/seaborn. Use when creating any figure for a conference paper. |

Use when ingesting a paper or codebase into a structured, machine-executable knowledge package, building an ARA from scratch, or converting research outputs into a falsifiable, agent-traversable form. |
| 3 | [ara-research-manager](external/AI-Research-SKILLs/22-agent-native-research-artifact/research-manager/SKILL.md) | Records research provenance as a post-task epilogue, scanning conversation history at the end of a coding or research session to extract decisions, experiments, dead ends, claims, heuristics, and pivots, and writing them into the ara/ directory with user-vs-AI provenance tags. Use as a session epilogue - never during execution - to maintain a faithful, auditable trace of how a research project actually evolved. |

Six-step loop (intake -> audit -> claim-evidence check -> rewrite -> self-score -> cold-reader recheck) in three modes (audit-only / edit / author-voiced), 30 English + 28 Chinese patterns revised in 2026-10 against the ten most-starred humanizer skills, a protect-list, per-section edit caps, and hard rules that keep every number, coefficient and citation intact and never invent anchors. Two stdlib scripts - aigc_audit.py (deterministic rule scan; fidelity gate for numbers, citations, negation and verb strength; draft-retention metrics) and provenance_scrub.py (CJK-safe invisible-character, look-alike and whitespace cleaning; docx / figure / PDF / zip metadata incl. | 377 | [Full-empirical-analysis-skill](external/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/plugins/empirical-analysis-python/skills/pipeline/SKILL.md) | Classical end-to-end empirical analysis workflow in the traditional Python econometric stack - pandas + numpy + scipy + statsmodels + linearmodels + pyfixest + rdrobust + econml + causalml + matplotlib/seaborn. **Defaults to economics empirical-paper style** (AER / QJE / AEJ) - every run produces a publication-ready output set with a multi-column regression table (M1->M6 progressive controls/FE) as the centerpiece, plus Table 1 (descriptives), mechanism / heterogeneity / robustness tables, and event-study + coefficient + trend figures. Covers the full 8-step pipeline an applied economist or quantitative social scientist runs on every paper - (1) data cleaning, (2) variable construction & transformation, (3) descriptive statistics & Table 1, (4) statistical diagnostic tests, (5) baseline empirical modeling, (6) robustness battery, (7) further analysis (mechanism, heterogeneity, mediation, moderation), (8) publication-ready tables & figures. **Also covers two parallel domain modes that share the same 8-step scaffolding** - **Mode A - Epidemiology / public health** (target-trial emulation via `zepid` / hand-rolled `pandas`, IPTW + g-formula + TMLE doubly-robust triplet via `zepid` / `econml` / `lifelines`, Mendelian randomization via `pymr` / `mrtool` (or `rpy2` -> `MendelianRandomization`/`TwoSampleMR`), KM / AFT / Cox survival via `lifelines`, E-value sensitivity, principal stratification - STROBE / TRIPOD reporting), and **Mode B - ML causal inference** (DML via `econml.dml` / `doubleml`, S/T/X/R/DR meta-learners via `econml.metalearners` / `causalml`, causal forest via `econml.grf` / `causalml`, Dragonnet / TARNet / CEVAE neural causal via `causalml`, BCF via `pymc-bart` / `bcf-py`, matrix completion, CATE distribution + policy tree via `econml.policy` / `policytree-py`, off-policy evaluation, conformal causal via `mapie`, fairness audit via `fairlearn`, DAG learning via `causal-learn` / `cdt` / LLM-assisted). Prescribes which library to reach for at each step, shows the canonical code, and links to deeper `references/` files for variant-specific patterns. Use when the user asks for a **complete empirical analysis** in Python, wants to replicate an applied-economics paper from scratch, needs a reproducible workflow that is NOT opinionated on any single vertical package (contrast with StatsPAI), wants explicit control over every estimator and diagnostic, or asks "how do I write a full empirical pipeline in Python?". Also triggers when the user names a specific classical step in isolation - "winsorize at 1/99%", "run Breusch-Pagan", "build a Table 1 balance table", "do a placebo test", "event study plot", "mediation analysis" - and wants it wired into the broader pipeline. Mode A triggers on "target trial emulation", "IPTW", "TMLE", "Mendelian randomization", "STROBE", "公共健康", "流行病学". Use when the user asks for a complete R empirical analysis, wants a tidyverse-style reproducible R script / Quarto workflow, prefers fixest over reghdfe, needs the R counterpart to StatsPAI / 00.1 / 00.2, or names a specific R step in isolation ("feols with cluster", "MatchIt nearest neighbor", "bacondecomp in R", "gtsummary table 1", "modelsummary to Word"). Compile script supports pdflatex, xelatex, lualatex with auto-detection, latexmk backend, texfot log filtering, PDF/A output, and verbosity control (--verbose/--quiet). | 711 | [phack-detection](external/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/skills/73-brycewang-p-hacking-skills/skills/06-phack-detection/SKILL.md) | Test whether a body of reported results shows p-hacking, selective reporting or selection between stages, using the p-curve battery and the threshold and across-stages tests of Adda, Decker and Ottaviani (2020). Implements the binomial, Fisher, Stouffer and least-concave-majorant monotonicity tests of Elliott, Kudrin and Wuethrich (2022); threshold-bunching tests calibrated against a smooth counterfactual density; a Cattaneo-Jansson-Ma density-jump test and a right-side spike test that tell a level shift (results withheld) from a spike (results pushed across); the phase II vs phase III comparison and the selective-continuation decomposition; and Simonsohn's p-curve power estimate. Use when asked whether a literature, a journal, a research design, an author, a registry or a set of agent runs shows evidence of p-hacking, publication bias or selective continuation. |
| 712 | [phack-eval-harness](external/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/skills/73-brycewang-p-hacking-skills/skills/08-eval-harness/SKILL.md) | Run and score the agent p-hacking benchmark. Composes prompt cells across research framing and significance pressure, drives an agent through them, and scores each run into a P-Hacking Intensity index decomposed into selection, search breadth, estimate inflation, inference gap, pre-registration departure and disclosure. Use when benchmarking whether an AI agent p-hacks, comparing models or tool stacks on statistical integrity, scoring a single agent analysis run, or designing an evaluation of statistical guardrails. |
| 713 | [phack-immunization](external/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/skills/73-brycewang-p-hacking-skills/skills/07-phack-immunization/SKILL.md) | Make an empirical analysis robust to specification search, before or after the fact. Covers pre-registration and pre-analysis plans encoded as design cards, specification-curve reporting with the Simonsohn joint tests, Romano-Wolf and effective-multiplicity corrections, full-procedure null calibration including for sequential searches, the distance-from-pre-registration diagnostic, split-sample and holdout designs, and the auto-generated honest report. Use when asked how to protect an analysis against p-hacking, how to correct inference after a search has happened, how to write a pre-analysis plan, or how to report a multiverse credibly. |
| 714 | [phack-polyglot](external/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/skills/73-brycewang-p-hacking-skills/skills/10-phack-polyglot/SKILL.md) | Run the instrumented specification search in the user's own statistical language - Stata (reghdfe, ivreghdfe, rdrobust, did2s), R (fixest, rdrobust, did2s), Python (statsmodels, linearmodels) or StatsPAI - and bring the results back into the audit, null calibration and honest report. Exports the enumerated grid as a language-neutral specs table plus a generated runner, ingests the runner's ledger, replays the null draws in that language, and reports cross-language parity. Use when an analysis or an agent evaluation must happen in Stata, R or StatsPAI rather than the Python engine, when a p-hacked result was produced in one of those languages and needs auditing on its own footing, when reading Stata / R / StatsPAI code for search and disclosure signals, or when checking that the engine's numbers agree with a reference implementation. |
| 715 | [phack-router](external/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/skills/73-brycewang-p-hacking-skills/skills/00-phack-router/SKILL.md) | Entry point for the p-hacking skills suite. Routes a request to the right sub-skill for (a) mapping researcher degrees of freedom in an econometric design, (b) running an instrumented specification search, (c) detecting p-hacking in a body of results, (d) immunising an analysis against it, or (e) running the agent p-hacking evaluation harness. | 4 | [orca-cli](external/orca/skills/orca-cli/SKILL.md) | Operate Orca-managed worktrees, folder contexts, terminals, repos, automations, worktree comments, and Orca's embedded browser through the `orca` CLI. Use when the user says "$orca-cli", "Orca worktree", "child worktree", "spawn codex/claude in a worktree", "read/wait/send Orca terminal", "handoff" / "handover" / "give this to another agent", "ask/tell/message another agent" (including an agent on a paired Orca server), or "Orca browser". Also use for opening, testing, screenshotting, or checking how a local or external web page renders while working in Orca, unless the user or project names another browser tool; plain HTTP or API calls (curl, fetch) do not need it. Prefer it over raw git worktree, ad hoc PTYs, or Computer Use when Orca state is involved. Use Computer Use only when a visible window needs GUI control that a CLI, filesystem, or API cannot do. |

Prefer a programmatic path (shell, filesystem, git, HTTP, existing CLIs) whenever it can complete the task. Use only when an app window needs GUI control those cannot reach. Do not use for Orca's embedded browser (`orca-cli`). |

Unless the user asks for Orca workers or a named agent, searches, reads and research in your workspace stay with your own subagents, even in parallel. Use `orca-cli` to hand work off with nothing expected back ("hand off", "handover", "give this to another agent", "another worktree"), and for terminal control, shell commands, worktree management, and reading or waiting on terminals. |

Use when the user mentions AlphaGenome, AlphaGenome Atlas, AVI or AlphaGenome Variant Impact, DeepMind variant effect prediction, or wants to prioritise or mechanistically interpret non-coding, regulatory, splicing, enhancer, promoter, or chromatin-accessibility effects of SNVs from a VCF, credible set, or region. | 43 | [s01-san-gui](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s01-san-gui/SKILL.md) | '【何时调用】想从自身或团队经验中提炼可复用认知、做复盘，或把反复发生的现象升级为背后因果/法则时。触发词：从经验找规律/找本质（find rules from experience）、复盘（retrospective）、底层逻辑（root cause）。 【何时不调用】仅记事实、直接学他人结论、或只需套用已有模型时不必走三归；高精确度归因需配合 s02 控制变量法。' |
| 44 | [s02-control-variable](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s02-control-variable/SKILL.md) | '【何时调用】归因时想提高准确率、判断"是它导致结果还是只是相关"，或做 A/B 测试、对照实验、拆解多因素纠缠（如业绩下滑因谁）时。触发词：控制变量（control variables）、是因果还是相关（causal or correlated）、做个对照测试（run a controlled test）。 【何时不调用】因素不可观测/不可操控（如人性底层动机）只能靠向外求补充；只需定性理解、不求精确归因时不必大动干戈。' |
| 45 | [s03-memory](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s03-memory/SKILL.md) | '【何时调用】学完就忘、要记术语/外语/考点、备考或准备演讲需稳定长时记忆，或用笔记软件设计高效复习节奏时。触发词：记不住/学完就忘（can''t remember）、怎么高效复习（how to review efficiently）、间隔重复（spaced repetition）。 【何时不调用】知识只是一次性信息无需长时记忆；或任务纯理解/创造、记忆非瓶颈时，精力应放思考而非重复。' |
| 46 | [s04-understanding](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s04-understanding/SKILL.md) | '当你读不懂/记不住却说不清"为什么"、听懂了却不会用、或要快速入门陌生学科时调用。不要在纯机械记忆(归 s03)、消遣阅读、或领域完全无旧知且无框架可用时调用。触发词: "看不懂/听不懂"、"懂了但用不出来"、"这书太难了"、"I don''t get it"、"I can recite but can''t apply"。' |
| 47 | [s05-review-model](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s05-review-model/SKILL.md) | '当连续在同类事上踩坑、想把一次成功/失败沉淀成可复用方法、或要根治反复出现的问题时调用; 当用户遇到外部不可控失败、想"复盘"却易虚假归因时, 也应激活以引导只沉淀应对法而非强行因果。不要在单次偶发事件、纯外部不可控问题、或仅想宣泄情绪时调用。触发词: "又犯同样的错了"、"这次成功纯靠运气"、"怎么老在这种事上栽跟头"、"I keep making the same mistake"、"turn this into a method"。' |
| 48 | [s06-six-step-reading](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s06-six-step-reading/SKILL.md) | '当读完非虚构书却说不出收获、想用模型树把书变成自己的模型、或读书笔记只是抄书无独立见解时调用。不要在纯文学/虚构体验阅读、消遣阅读、或已烂熟领域逐句硬拆时调用。触发词: "读完了但没记住什么"、"怎么做读书笔记"、"怎么把书变成自己的"、"how to extract models from a book"、"I just copy quotes"。' |
| 49 | [s07-model-definition](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s07-model-definition/SKILL.md) | '用于判断"某个规律/经验/四字总结"是否算一个真正可用的模型，以及如何正确使用模型。 何时调用：①用户问"我提炼的X算模型吗？"②用户把知识强行压成四字就当模型 ③用户用一句格言硬套所有情境、遇到冲突模型无所适从 ④用户想检验自己总结的法则能否揭示因果。 何时不调用：只需记忆孤立事实、或问题本身只涉及单点技巧（用 s04/s03）。 关键 trigger（中英双写）："这算模型吗？/ Is this a real model?"、"模型不就是四字吗？/ A model is just four characters?"、"为什么A和B矛盾？/ Why do these models conflict?"。' |
| 50 | [s08-knowledge-system](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s08-knowledge-system/SKILL.md) | '用于从零搭建一个领域的知识体系（主题/框架/结构），并理解"知识体系本质=知识之间的关系"。 何时调用：①用户想搭"XX领域"知识体系 ②用户学了很多却混乱、记不住、用不上 ③用户用双链笔记但说不清关系 ④用户做主题阅读想串起多本书。 何时不调用：只需记孤立事实、或问题只涉及单点理解（用 s04/s03）。 关键 trigger（中英双写）："如何搭建XX知识体系？/ How do I build a knowledge system for X?"、"学了很多但很乱/用不上 / I learned a lot but it''s a mess"、"这些笔记连不起来 / My notes don''t connect"。' |
| 51 | [s09-first-principles](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s09-first-principles/SKILL.md) | '用于逼近事物本质、获取并运用"第一原理"（绝对为真的起点知识），核心思维是极致求根+求真。 何时调用：①用户想看透某事物本质/根源 ②用户做重大决策（创业/战略/择业）想基于根本而非经验 ③用户习惯类比同行却难以创新 ④用户想把自己知识体系收敛到本质。 何时不调用：只是一次性具体技巧、或问题用普通模型已够（用 s07）；纯记忆事实（用 s03）。 关键 trigger（中英双写）："这件事的本质是什么？/ What''s the essence of this?"、"大家都这么做但我想要根本解法 / I want a first-principles solution"、"如何从根本上降本？/ How to cut cost from first principles?"。' |
| 52 | [s10-transfer-create](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s10-transfer-create/SKILL.md) | '何时调用：当你需要把已掌握的知识迁移到新领域、想做出真正创新、或要做高确定性决策时使用；含用户想类比/照搬/校仿他人或竞品做法（以为照着做就行）时，激活本 skill 以纠正"真迁移≠简单类比，先抽象到原理再演绎"；用户想创新但自感原理不足时，激活以判停并引导先去 s09 积累第一原理。 何时不调用：当你连相关领域的底层原理都还没掌握时（此时应先去 s09 积累第一原理，否则只是类比式套用，不是真迁移）。 关键 trigger（中英双写）： - "这个方法能用到别的地方吗" / Can this be applied to other domains? - "怎么才能真正创新/创造" / How to genuinely create something new? - "怎么做才是对的" / What is the right decision here?' |
| 53 | [s11-model-tree-four-steps](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s11-model-tree-four-steps/SKILL.md) | 用"外接大脑 / 六步提炼 / 存量标准化 / 形成体系"四步把一本书真正吃透。 |
| 54 | [s12-prune-model-tree](external/deeptutor-eduhub/s12-prune-model-tree/SKILL.md) | '何时调用：当你的模型树已建起、出现重复/混乱/想深入本质，或到了定期（每年）大修剪时使用；含用户想"一次修剪完美、之后不管"时，激活以纠正"修剪是终身迭代"。 何时不调用：当树还几乎是空的（先去 s11 总装），或你因"怕提炼错"而不敢动手时（错误可后期修）。 关键 trigger（中英双写）： - "我的知识体系好乱、模型重复" / My knowledge system is messy with duplicate models. - "是时候做深度思考/大扫除了" / Time for deep pruning / a big cleanup. - "怎么才能看透本质" / How do I see through to the essence?' |
